TL;DR: 本文通过结合体内成像基因编码乙酰胆碱/钙传感器与运动记录，研究了小鼠第二周龄（睁眼前）初级视觉皮层中乙酰胆碱动力学、神经元活动与行为状态的关系。发现乙酰胆碱释放紧密追踪身体运动而非视觉刺激，升高期伴随降低的神经元成对相关性、更长且时间抖动更大的网络事件以及V1平均活动的短暂降低；化学遗传抑制基底前脑胆碱能神经元可增加网络事件内部同步性并部分逆转运动相关的V1活动降低，表明乙酰胆碱是视觉经验开始前调控发育期视觉皮层自发活动的状态依赖调节因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑发育过程中同步化和去同步化的自发活动模式为皮层（如视觉皮层）处理外部感觉信息做准备，但这些早期活动模式的神经调质调控机制尚不清楚。乙酰胆碱在成年个体中能追踪行为状态并调节视觉皮层活动，但其在发育皮层中的功能鲜有探索。

Method: 在小鼠视觉皮层中结合体内成像基因编码乙酰胆碱传感器与钙传感器，并同步进行运动记录，表征睁眼前第二个出生后周内胆碱能动力学、神经元活动与行为状态的关系。采用化学遗传学手段抑制基底前脑胆碱能神经元以检验其对网络同步性和V1活动的影响。

Result: 乙酰胆碱在初级视觉皮层的释放紧密追踪身体运动，但单独视觉刺激不诱发其释放。乙酰胆碱升高期与神经元成对相关性降低、更长的网络事件及更大的时间抖动、V1平均活动的短暂降低相关。化学遗传抑制基底前脑胆碱能神经元增加了网络事件的内部同步性并部分逆转了运动相关的V1活动降低。

Conclusion: 基底前脑胆碱能输入是发育期视觉皮层中这种胆碱能调控的来源，乙酰胆碱在视觉经验开始之前即作为状态依赖性调节因子调控发育期视觉皮层的自发活动。

Abstract: Synchronized and desynchronized patterns of spontaneous activity during brain development prepare cortical circuits such as the visual cortex for processing external sensory information. Yet the neuromodulatory mechanisms that regulate these early activity patterns remain poorly understood. One such neuromodulator is acetylcholine, which tracks behavioral states and modulates visual cortical activity in adults, but whose function in the developing cortex is largely unexplored. Here, we combined in vivo imaging of genetically encoded acetylcholine and calcium sensors with movement recordings to characterize the relationship between cholinergic dynamics, neuronal activity, and behavioral states in the mouse visual cortex during the second postnatal week, before eye opening. We found that acetylcholine release in the primary visual cortex (V1) closely tracked body movements but was not evoked by visual stimulation alone. Periods of elevated acetylcholine were associated with reduced pairwise neuronal correlations, longer network events with greater temporal jitter, and a transient reduction in mean V1 activity. Chemogenetic inhibition of basal forebrain cholinergic neurons increased the internal synchrony of network events and partially reversed the movement-associated reduction in V1 activity, identifying basal forebrain input as a source of this cholinergic modulation. These findings establish acetylcholine as a state-dependent regulator of spontaneous activity in the developing visual cortex before visual experience begins.

</details>


### [90] [Bulk medial prefrontal cortex activity comports with a functional bipartition between salience detection and movement gain](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.03.18.712525v2)
*Hou, S.,Ramirez, L. A.,Mitten, E. H.,Wiaderkiewicz, J.,Narvaez Guzman, M.,Wang, H.,Glover, E. J.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究通过在雄性和雌性大鼠上进行体内mPFC钙成像，结合奖赏性和厌恶性巴甫洛夫任务及持续无偏行为监测，发现短潜伏期的整体mPFC活动仅报告显著性（salience），与效价、价值或奖励预测误差（RPE）无关。进一步通过双向光遗传学验证，这种显著性无关的mPFC活动以与效价和任务无关的方式调节泛化运动。结果挑战了mPFC整体活动统一表征认知过程（包括RPE信号）的普遍观点，支持将功能划分为反应性显著检测和运动增益动态调节的二分模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励预测误差（RPE）被认为由全脑神经状态实现以驱动学习和适应性反应，内侧前额叶皮层（mPFC）是参与RPE计算的关键脑区之一。然而，mPFC的RPE处理是否因效价、价值和符号不同而存在差异尚不清楚，本研究旨在阐明这一问题。

Method: 在雄性和雌性大鼠中进行体内mPFC钙成像，采用奖赏性或厌恶性巴甫洛夫任务以诱发RPE，并结合持续、无偏的行为监测；使用体内双向光遗传学对关键发现进行验证。

Result: 短潜伏期的整体mPFC活动独立于效价、价值或RPE，仅报告显著性信息。出人意料的是，体内双向光遗传学证实，与显著性无关的mPFC活动以与效价和任务无关的方式调节泛化性运动。

Conclusion: 这些结果挑战了mPFC整体活动统一表征认知过程（包括RPE信号）的传统观念，转而支持一种功能性二分框架：一方面进行对整合显著性的反应性检测，另一方面对运动增益进行动态调节。

Abstract: Reward prediction error (RPE) is a key function putatively realized by brain-wide neural states to drive learning and adaptive responding. The medial prefrontal cortex (mPFC) is among the many areas canonically implicated in RPE computation. Yet, whether mPFC RPE processing differs as a function of valence, value, and sign is not well-understood. Here, we performed in vivo mPFC calcium imaging in male and female rats engaged in an appetitive or aversive Pavlovian task designed to elicit RPE, along with continuous, unbiased behavioral monitoring. We found that short-latency bulk mPFC activity reports salience independent of valence, value, or RPE. Surprisingly, as subsequently validated with in vivo bidirectional optogenetics, we show that salience-null mPFC activity modulates generalized movement in a valence- and task-agnostic fashion. Together, these results challenge the pervasive notion of a unitary, region-wide representation of cognitive processes, including RPE signaling, by bulk mPFC activity, and instead, support a functional bipartition schema combining reactive detection of integrated salience and dynamic modulation of movement gain.

</details>


### [91] [Tunable neuronal microenvironments drive distinct functional phenotypes in human iPSCs-derived dopaminergic neurons](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.08.28.744416v1)
*Daou, B.,Rodriguez Sanchez, L.,Sartori, P.,Carina Luque, G.,Prato, M.,Alegret, N.,Alonso-Martin, S.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究合成了两种可负载碳纳米管（CNT）的壳聚糖水凝胶系统作为可调控平台培养人iPSC来源的多巴胺能神经元，通过钙成像和单细胞计算分析发现水凝胶的超分子结构、水合状态及理化性质可差异性地调控神经元放电动力学和同步性，即使细胞来源相同也能产生不同活动表型；CNT的掺入增强了局部水凝胶致密化，形成独特的活跃神经元表型，表明可调生物材料可作为控制神经元网络状态的微环境平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经元异质性对复杂神经回路的信息处理和网络活动至关重要，成年神经元中这种异质性源自包括细胞外环境在内的多种已知和未知因素。尽管神经元已在三维基底上培养，但微环境对其放电活动的影响仍知之甚少，因此需要可控平台来系统研究微环境线索如何塑造神经元功能表型。

Method: 合成了两种不同理化性质的壳聚糖水凝胶系统，均可掺入碳纳米管以促进神经界面连接；将人iPSC来源的多巴胺能神经元种植于其上，结合钙成像与计算单细胞分析（K-means聚类）对神经元放电活动进行分类和研究。

Result: 不同水凝胶的超分子组织、水合状态和理化性质的差异会差异化地调控神经元放电动力学和同步性，使相同细胞来源的神经元产生不同的活动表型（包括爆发放电不规则神经元、规则网络参与者、低活动/静息神经元）；CNT掺入增强了局部水凝胶的致密化，产生独特的活跃神经元表型。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了可调生物材料作为调控神经元网络状态的微环境因子，为理解不同微环境线索在促进神经元异质性和功能表型中的相互作用提供了见解，相关发现对神经发育、神经退行性疾病建模具有重要潜在意义。

Abstract: Neuronal heterogeneity is a defining feature of complex neural circuits, where local differences in firing patterns, activity levels, and temporal dynamics shape information processing and emergent network activity. In adult neurons, this heterogeneity arises from a variety of known and unknown factors including the extracellular environment. Although neurons have been cultured on three-dimensional substrates, the effect of the microenvironments on their firing activity remains poorly understood. Here, we have synthesized two chitosan hydrogel systems seeded with human iPSCs-derived dopaminergic neurons as tunable platforms to control neuronal microenvironments. Both systems were formulated with the ability to incorporate carbon nanotubes (CNT), thus promoting neural interfacing. Calcium imaging combined with computational single-cell analysis demonstrated that supramolecular organization, hydration state, and general physicochemical properties differentially bias neuronal firing dynamics and synchrony leading to the emergence of distinct activity phenotypes despite identical cellular origin. These activity profiles were clustered through K-means and assigned to specific phenotypes including bursting irregular neurons, regular network contributors, or less active/quiescent neurons. Furthermore, CNTs incorporation enhanced local hydrogel compaction, resulting in unique active neuronal phenotypes, highlighting the potential of CNTs to modulate local cellular microenvironments. These findings establish tunable biomaterials as microenvironment contenders for controlling neuronal network state while giving insights on the interplay of different cues in promoting neuronal heterogeneity and functional phenotype relevant to neurodevelopment, neurodegeneration, and disease modelling.

</details>


### [92] [Projection-dependent VGluT1 and VGluT2 expression and terminal morphology in mouse visual circuits](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.08.31.748331v1)
*Laliberte, G.,Tremblay-Laliberte, R.,Boire, D.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究通过逆向与顺行示踪结合VGluT1/VGluT2免疫荧光标记，系统表征了小鼠视觉系统多种通路中谷氨酸能突触末梢的转运体表型，发现VGluT异构体与投射类别存在关联但并非投射方向或驱动-调制功能的绝对分子指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGluT1和VGluT2被提出分别作为下行调制性通路和上行驱动样通路的分子标记，但这种对应关系很少在解剖学鉴定的投射通路中直接被验证，因此需要系统性检验。

Method: 结合顺行性Phaseolus vulgaris leucoagglutinin示踪，同时进行VGluT1和VGluT2免疫荧光标记，对成年小鼠初级/次级视觉皮层、外侧膝状体核、外侧后丘脑核和上丘来源的谷氨酸能末端进行表征，包括表型鉴定和形态计量分析。

Result: 2178个单标记末端中，VGluT1在皮层丘脑、皮层脑桥、皮层被盖、皮层纹状体和皮层-皮层反馈投射中占优势；而丘脑皮层、被盖丘脑、丘脑纹状体和被盖脑桥投射几乎均为VGluT2阳性。上行皮层-皮层投射仍以VGluT1为主，而下行被盖脑桥投射却完全为VGluT2阳性。2190个VGluT免疫反应末端中仅12个呈双标记共定位。形态分析显示VGluT异构体与投射方向之间存在交互作用。

Conclusion: VGluT表型与小鼠视觉系统的投射类别存在关联，可作为通路组织的有效标记，但转运体身份或末端大小均不能单独作为投射方向或驱动-调制功能的恒定分子指标。

Abstract: Vesicular glutamate transporters 1 and 2 (VGluT1 and VGluT2) exhibit largely complementary distributions and have been proposed as molecular markers of descending modulatory and ascending driver-like pathways, respectively. However, this correspondence has rarely been tested directly in anatomically identified projections. We combined anterograde Phaseolus vulgaris leucoagglutinin tracing with simultaneous VGluT1 and VGluT2 immunofluorescence to characterize glutamatergic boutons arising from the primary and secondary visual cortices, lateral geniculate nucleus, lateral posterior thalamic nucleus, and superior colliculus in adult mice. Among 2,178 singly labelled boutons, VGluT1 predominated in corticothalamic, corticopontine, corticotectal, corticostriatal, and corticocortical feedback projections. Conversely, thalamocortical, tectothalamic, thalamostriatal, and tectopontine projections were almost exclusively VGluT2-positive. Projection direction did not fully predict transporter phenotype: ascending corticocortical projections remained predominantly VGluT1-positive, whereas the descending tectopontine projection was exclusively VGluT2-positive. Only 12 of 2,190 VGluT-immunoreactive boutons exhibited detectable VGluT1/VGluT2 colocalization. Morphometric analyses further revealed an interaction between VGluT isoform and projection direction. Within ascending projections, VGluT2-positive boutons and puncta were larger than their VGluT1-positive counterparts, whereas no isoform-related difference in bouton area was detected within descending projections. These findings demonstrate an association between VGluT phenotype and projection class in the mouse visual system. VGluT isoforms therefore provide informative markers of pathway organization, but neither transporter identity nor terminal size alone constitutes an invariant molecular indicator of projection direction or driver-modulator function.

</details>


### [93] [Posterior parietal cortex guides sensorimotor associative learning by linking sensation to distal action](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.04.19.719425v2)
*Zhu, Q.,Wang, Y.,Huang, Y.,Zhang, J.,Yang, X.,Li, A.,Li, S.,Guo, Z. V.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本文利用全皮层单细胞钙成像追踪约16,000个神经元，发现外侧后顶叶皮层（PPC-rl）在触觉刺激与延迟舔舐的关联学习中充当桥梁，通过持续的延迟期活动放大低维通信子空间来维持感觉-运动耦合，从而支持延迟关联学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感觉运动关联学习在感觉线索和运动反应之间存在时间延迟时会受到挑战，因为Hebbian可塑性所需的共活动性受限。作者希望识别出能够跨越这一时间鸿沟、维持感觉与延迟运动之间联系的皮层机制。

Method: 采用全皮层单细胞分辨率钙成像同时追踪感觉、运动和联合皮层中约16,000个神经元，结合光遗传学沉默实验以及生物合理性网络建模，研究了触觉刺激与延迟舔舐关联学习过程中PPC-rl的作用及其神经机制。

Result: PPC-rl在学习的早期阶段表现出持续的延迟期活动，随着熟练度增加而减弱；光遗传沉默PPC-rl会减慢学习但不会损害专家运动执行；学习增强了皮层内和跨皮层的感受运动耦合，PPC-rl通过放大低维通信子空间来对齐感受运动的共波动；网络模型显示PPC-rl的持续活动、子空间对齐和奖励门控的Hebbian可塑性协同支持延迟关联。

Conclusion: 研究发现PPC-rl是基于皮层的感觉运动延迟关联学习的关键枢纽，它通过在学习过程中维持时间连续性将感觉信息与延迟运动行为联系起来，揭示了延迟关联任务的皮层计算机制。

Abstract: Sensorimotor associative learning is challenged when sensory cues and motor responses are separated by a delay, limiting the coactivity required for Hebbian plasticity. Here we identify the rostro-lateral posterior parietal cortex (PPC-rl) as a cortical hub that bridges tactile stimuli and delayed licking during learning. Using cortex-wide calcium imaging with single-cell resolution to track ~16,000 neurons simultaneously across sensory, motor, and association cortices, we find that PPC-rl exhibits sustained delay-epoch activity early in learning that diminishes with expertise. Optogenetic silencing slows learning without impairing expert motor execution. Learning strengthens intra- and inter-areal sensorimotor coupling, and PPC-rl mediates this process by amplifying a low-dimensional communication subspace that aligns sensorimotor co-fluctuations. A biologically plausible network model shows that PPC-rl persistence, subspace alignment, and reward-gated Hebbian plasticity synergize to support delayed association. These findings uncover a PPC-rl based cortical mechanism that maintains temporal continuity to link sensation with distal action during learning.

</details>


### [94] [tACS increases alpha burst duration in a frequency- and montage-specific manner](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.08.31.747228v1)
*Rodriguez Larios, J.,Ragone, A.,Chauhan, S.,Koizumi, K.,Di Bernardi Luft, C.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究通过2×2实验设计（后部或额顶刺激 × 10 Hz或6 Hz）外加无刺激对照组，在158名健康参与者中进行持续注意力任务，发现后部10 Hz经颅交流电刺激（tACS）能显著增加alpha振荡功率，其机制是延长alpha振荡爆发（burst）的持续时间而非增加其幅度，且这种效应具有刺激位置和频率特异性，但不伴随行为表现或静息态认知的显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究表明后部区域的10 Hz tACS能普遍增强alpha（7-15 Hz）振荡，但该效应在刺激频率和位置上的特异性及其对认知的影响尚不清楚。作者旨在厘清tACS对神经振荡和认知的假想效应是否特异地局限于alpha频率范围的后部刺激。

Method: 采用2×2析因设计（后部或额顶-顶叶刺激 × 10 Hz或6 Hz刺激）外加无刺激对照组，共158名健康参与者随机分配至对照组或四个主动刺激组之一。所有参与者在刺激前后均进行脑电图（EEG）记录，并完成阿姆斯特丹静息状态问卷，期间执行持续注意力任务。

Result: 相较于对照组，仅接受后部10 Hz刺激的组在刺激后alpha功率显著增加，且这一增加源于alpha振荡爆发持续时间的延长而非其幅度的提升。活跃刺激组与对照组在行为表现或静息状态认知上均未发现显著差异。

Conclusion: tACS以刺激电极布局（montage）和频率特异性的方式延长了alpha振荡爆发的持续时间，但这种神经调控并未伴随刺激期间持续注意力或刺激后静息状态认知的显著改变。

Abstract: Transcranial alternating current stimulation (tACS) can experimentally manipulate neural oscillations in humans non-invasively. Previous studies consistently suggest that 10 Hz tACS in posterior regions leads to a general increase in alpha (7-15 Hz) oscillations. However, the specificity of this effect regarding stimulation frequency and location as well as its consequences on cognition are still unclear. We therefore aimed to investigate whether the putative effects of tACS on neural oscillations and cognition are specific to posterior stimulation in the alpha frequency range. We tested these effects using a 2 x 2 design (posterior or fronto-parietal stimulation, at 10 Hz or 6 Hz) plus a no-stimulation control group, during a sustained attention task.158 healthy participants were randomly allocated to control group (no stimulation) or one of the four active groups. All participants underwent an Electroencephalography recording and completed the Amsterdam Resting State Questionnaire before and after stimulation. Our results show that, relative to the control group, alpha power increases significantly after stimulation for the group receiving posterior 10 Hz stimulation and that this was explained by an increase in alpha oscillatory bursts duration rather than their amplitude. On the other hand, no significant differences were found between active and controls groups in behavioural performance or resting state cognition. Together, our findings demonstrate that tACS increases the duration of alpha oscillatory bursts in a montage- and frequency-specific manner and that this modulation is not accompanied by significant changes in sustained attention during stimulation or resting state cognition after stimulation.

</details>


### [95] [Universality Meets Cultural Nuances: Affective Responses to Rhythmic and Harmonic Complexity Across Cultures](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.08.31.748217v1)
*Klarlund, M.,Hua, S.,Matthews, T.,Stupacher, J.,Brattico, E.,Du, Y.,Vuust, P.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究通过两个在线实验，比较中国与丹麦听众对节奏与和声复杂性的"愉悦感"和"想动感"评分，检验groove（音乐引发运动冲动的愉悦感）的倒U型曲线是否具有文化普遍性。结果显示两组对节奏复杂性均呈现倒U型反应，支持预测编码理论的普遍性，但丹麦听众表现出更陡峭的曲线即更强的情绪分化能力；和声复杂性未出现倒U型模式，且中国人未显现增强的和声敏感性。研究揭示文化暴露对音乐情感体验的调节作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于groove与节奏、和声复杂性之间倒U型关系的研究主要基于西方听众，其文化普适性尚不明确。鉴于已有研究表明中国听众对音高与和声具有更高的敏感性，研究者预测中国听众会对和声复杂性反应更强、对节奏复杂性反应更弱，旨在检验这一跨文化差异假设。

Method: 开展两项在线实验，以中国和丹麦参与者分别代表东西方音乐传统。实验1同时操纵节奏与和声复杂性两个维度，测量参与者的"愉悦感"与"想要移动"评分；实验2聚焦于节奏复杂性，采用生态效度更高的刺激材料并设置更精细的梯度层级，再次比较两组参与者的反应曲线。

Result: 实验1中，两组参与者对节奏复杂性均表现出倒U型反应，但丹麦参与者的曲线更陡峭，表明更强的情绪区分能力；和声复杂性未出现倒U型模式，中国参与者亦未显示增强的和声敏感性。实验2中，丹麦人再次表现出对节奏变化的更高敏感性（倒U曲线曲率更大），但两组峰值反应无显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持了预测性反应（倒U型曲线）对节奏复杂性具有文化普遍性，但同时强调文化暴露在塑造音乐情感体验中起到调节作用。预测编码框架下的groove机制可能是跨文化共通的，而具体的情感分化程度受文化背景影响。

Abstract: The pleasurable urge to move to music - known as groove - has been linked to rhythmic and harmonic complexity via an inverted-U relationship, consistent with predictive coding theory of music. This link has been demonstrated mainly in Western listeners, leaving its cultural generality unclear. Given reports of heightened pitch and harmony sensitivity in Chinese listeners, we predicted stronger responses to harmonic complexity and weaker responses to rhythmic complexity compared with Western listeners. We conducted two online experiments measuring "pleasure" and "wanting to move" ratings for musical excerpts varying in rhythmic and harmonic complexity, comparing Chinese and Danish participants as representatives of Eastern and Western traditions. In Experiment 1, which manipulated both dimensions, both groups showed inverted-U responses to rhythmic complexity, but Danish participants exhibited a sharper curve, indicating greater affective differentiation. No inverted-U pattern emerged for harmonic complexity, and Chinese participants did not show enhanced harmonic sensitivity. Experiment 2 focused on rhythmic complexity using ecologically valid stimuli with finer gradations. Again, Danes showed greater sensitivity to rhythmic variation, reflected in a sharper curvature of their inverted U responses, though peak responses did not differ between groups. Our findings support the universality of predictive responses to rhythmic complexity, while highlighting the modulatory role of cultural exposure in shaping musical affective experiences.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Efficient Constant Optimization for Symbolic Regression with GPU-Accelerated Tree-Based Genetic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03352)
*Hao Mao,Xu Tony Liu,Shuai Lu,Peng Zhao,Wenzheng Jiang,Yuntian Chen*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出一种GPU驻留的批量Levenberg-Marquardt求解器，用于符号回归中基于树的遗传编程的常量优化，在异构表达式树群体上以固定的群体级CUDA启动次数迭代，使用反向模式自动微分实现单次反向扫描装配每个树的雅可比矩阵，并通过双精度递送保护确保返回值不劣于初值。在A100上达到每秒5.1e5棵树，在GPU饱和配置下吞吐量约为64核EPYC 7763上Operon的9.9倍且保持fp64参考质量，集成到EvoGP后使18个构造问题中能恢复控制方程的从0个增至10个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常量优化能精炼符号回归中基于树的遗传编程候选表达式的数值系数，但其逐代计算成本导致现代GPU加速框架要么省略它，要么将其限制为轻量形式，因此需要一种高效的、可扩展至GPU并行范式的常量优化求解方法。

Method: 提出GPU驻留的批量Levenberg-Marquardt求解器：在结构异构的表达式树群体上执行常量优化，每迭代仅使用固定次数的群体级CUDA启动；利用反向模式自动微分在单次反向扫描中为每棵树组装雅可比矩阵，使每迭代的主导成本与每棵树的常量数量无关；并加入双精度递送保护机制确保返回的常量不劣于初值。

Result: 在早期世代群体上，该求解器在NVIDIA A100上可维持高达5.1e5棵树/秒的吞吐量；在GPU饱和基准配置下，其吞吐量约为64核EPYC 7763上Operon的9.9倍，同时匹配fp64参考质量。集成进EvoGP后，端到端搜索在18个构造问题中于10个成功恢复控制方程（初始EvoGP为0个）。

Conclusion: GPU驻留的批量LM求解器可高效解决符号回归遗传编程中常量优化的性能瓶颈，在保持数值质量的同时大幅提升吞吐量与端到端搜索能力，为GPU加速框架引入完整常量优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Constant optimization refines the numerical coefficients of candidate expressions in tree-based genetic programming for symbolic regression. But its per-generation cost has led modern GPU-accelerated frameworks to omit it or restrict it to lightweight forms. We present a GPU-resident, batched Levenberg--Marquardt solver that optimizes constants across a structurally heterogeneous population of expression trees using a fixed number of population-wide CUDA launches per iteration. Reverse-mode automatic differentiation assembles the per-tree Jacobian in one backward sweep, making the dominant per-iteration cost independent of the number of constants per tree, and a double-precision delivery guard guarantees that returned constants are never worse than their initial values. On early-generation populations, the solver sustains up to $5.1{\times}10^{5}$ trees per second on an NVIDIA A100; at a GPU-saturated benchmark configuration it delivers roughly $9.9{\times}$ the throughput of Operon running on a 64-core EPYC 7763, while matching fp64-reference quality. Integrated in-process into EvoGP, the solver enables end-to-end search to recover governing equations on $10$ of $18$ constructed problems versus 0 for stock EvoGP. Our code is at https://github.com/TensorConv/CuSR.

</details>


### [97] [Genetic Algorithms for Tractable Bayesian Network Fusion via Pre-Fusion Edge Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03724)
*Pablo Torrijos,José A. Gámez,José M. Puerta,Juan A. Aledo*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个共识框架，用于在多输入贝叶斯网络融合中优先保留共享结构并限制树宽，并设计遗传算法（含高级初始化、专用算子和定制适应度函数）实现该共识，实验表明其优于适应后的现有方法和贪心基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯网络融合需要在依赖保留与计算可行性之间权衡。无限制融合保留所有依赖但树宽过高影响推理扩展性；有限融合通过剪枝控制树宽，但可能过拟合输入特定噪声并丢失原始网络中的依赖。因此需要一个既能共享结构共识又能满足树宽约束的融合框架。

Method: 提出一个以输入网络间共享结构为优先、同时强制树宽约束的共识融合框架；设计了具有高级初始化策略、专用遗传算子和定制适应度函数的遗传算法；同时将现有方法适配到该问题，并实现贪心基线用于基准测试和进一步优化。

Result: 在合成和真实贝叶斯网络上的实验表明，所提出的遗传算法在融合质量上显著优于适配的现有方法和贪心基线。

Conclusion: 遗传算法驱动的共识框架能够有效实现在树宽约束下优先共享依赖结构的贝叶斯网络融合，兼顾结构共识与推理可扩展性，优于已有适应方法和简单基准。

Abstract: Bayesian Network (BN) fusion combines multiple input networks into a single structure, balancing dependency preservation with computational tractability. While unrestricted fusion retains all dependencies, it often results in overly complex networks with high treewidth, which affects inference scalability. Limited fusion mitigates this by pruning edges to control treewidth but risks overfitting to input-specific noise and omitting dependencies from the original BNs. This paper introduces a consensus framework that prioritizes shared structures among input networks while enforcing treewidth constraints, ensuring a good consensus. We propose genetic algorithms with advanced initialization, specialized operators, and a tailored fitness function. Additionally, we adapt existing methods to this problem and implement greedy baselines for benchmarking and further optimization. Experiments on synthetic and real-world BNs show the superiority of the proposed genetic algorithms over the adapted methods and greedy baselines.

</details>
